### 1. user (2026-07-07T16:57:05.533Z)

Add a validation-result cache keyed by video_id so a reupload does NOT re-run validation when the same content was already validated.

Problem (confirmed in backend/orchestration.py::_execute_processing): the only skip path is a WHOLE-PIPELINE cache (~lines 732-749) that fires only when the video is status=="processed" AND has cached_segments (i.e., a prior SUCCESSFUL segmentation). So after a SEGMENTATION FAILURE (validation passed, detection failed → no cached segments), a reupload of the SAME video_id re-runs the full validator suite from scratch (`registry.run_all_with_context`, ~line 754) — which on a heavy clip is minutes (e.g. ~6.5 min on the 5.3K 10-bit HEVC "Video 1"). Validation results ARE already persisted on the video record (add_video stores validation_results), but they're never used to short-circuit the validation STEP. This bit the owner repeatedly during the 2026-07-07 CUJ retries.

video_id = MD5 of the content (backend/utils/hashing.py::compute_video_id), so identical bytes → identical id. Design a cache: when a request's video_id already has a stored validation verdict computed under the SAME validator configuration, skip re-running the validators and reuse the stored result (pass → proceed to segmentation; fail → return the stored failure). Key must include a validator-config fingerprint (e.g. hash of the relevant validation.* config: min_blur_threshold, min_fps, min_resolution, scene-cut/relevance thresholds, pts gap) so that a config change correctly invalidates the cache. Provide an override to force re-validation (req.force already bypasses the pipeline cache — extend/mirror that). Store either on the video record (extend the existing validation_results with the config fingerprint + a schema/version) or a dedicated table.

Interplay: this composes with #513 (worker already skips re-validation on dispatch) and #512 (validators are faster) — together, a post-failure reupload should reach segmentation in ~0 validation time. Guardrails: don't serve a stale PASS if the validator thresholds changed; keep the content-hash keying so a different video (even same filename) never reuses another's verdict.

Acceptance: a second /api/process for the same video_id (same validator config) skips validation (assert via logs/timing + a unit test in tests/); a config change forces re-validation; req.force bypasses. Repo pre-merge gate (full suite + ruff + mypy + coverage), branch from origin/master. Related: task 'Speed up video validation', #513.

61	## Key decisions (don't relitigate)
62	- GPU resize MUST be affine `grid_sample` matching `cv2.warpAffine` — NOT `F.interpolate` (F1 0.58→0.07; only shows on hard clips). Locked by unit tests now.
63	- `padding_mode="zeros"` (cv2's BORDER_CONSTANT(0)), not "border" — matters only for non-16:9 sources; identical to the validated run on 16:9.
64	- PyNvVideoCodec (MIT) for GPU decode; TorchCodec couldn't load on the DL image.
65	- Score with SERVED preset (cap6+R4); raw (0.02,2.0,2.0) is degenerate.
66	- Default-OFF; nvdec/cpu detection caches never cross-replay (manifest keyed by decode_backend).
67	- WASB weights/NYCU-dataset license (WASB-C3) is a separate launch gate, not a perf blocker.

11	https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/513 (branch deleted on the remote).
12	
13	**Bug:** on the cloud-serving path the control-plane validates + only dispatches on PASS, but the
14	worker's `cmd_infer` RE-RAN validation with its OWN config (the control-plane's resolved `validation.*`
15	keys aren't forwarded — [[gpu-resident-modernization]]/#509 forwards only `skip_ai_handoff` + `wasb.*`).
16	Live CUJ 2026-07-07: a 5312x2988 clip PASSED at `min_blur_threshold=8.0`, worker REJECTED at default
17	20.0 → successful GPU dispatch, then `status=failed`.
18	
19	**Fix (skip-on-dispatch):** new `ComputeJobSpec.skip_validation` → `CloudRunCompute` emits a
20	`--skip-validation` worker flag → `cmd_infer` honors it (skips `run_all_with_context`) →
21	`_maybe_dispatch_remote` sets it True on every dispatch. Direct `worker infer` CLI still validates.
22	Gate: 1780 passed / cov 85.42% / ruff + mypy clean.
23	
24	**Rollout caveat (in the PR + README §2a):** `--skip-validation` is a NEW worker CLI arg — a
25	control-plane-ahead-of-worker deploy makes an OLD worker image argparse-abort (rc 2) BEFORE the
26	done-marker → dispatcher polls the full `remote_poll_max_wait_sec` (~65 min) then fails opaquely.
27	Deploy **worker image first**, together with the control plane (same coupling #507 has for
28	`--decode-backend`). Follow-up (task_4e8bc546) — make the dispatcher fail FAST on any worker
29	crash-before-marker by consulting the Cloud Run execution status — is **now running** in the main
30	checkout on branch `fix/cloud-run-exec-status-fastfail`.
31

### 6. user (2026-07-07T16:57:25.886Z)

1	---
2	name: validation-shared-decode-pass
3	description: "Validator frame sampling now shares ONE decode pass; pure grab()-through is SLOWER than seeks, use forward-hybrid"
4	metadata: 
5	  node_type: memory
6	  type: project
7	  originSessionId: d3f3b3dc-c648-47b3-8dc4-5a6d3292c1a7
8	---
9	
10	The three frame-decoding validators (`scene_cut`, `blur`, `relevance` in
11	`backend/pipeline/validators/`) used to each open their OWN `cv2.VideoCapture`
12	and pull samples via per-sample `CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES` seeks — ~125 random seeks
13	across 3 decode opens. On long-GOP 4K GoPro clips on the CPU-only control-plane
14	this made "Running validations" take **6m47s** on a 1.41 GiB clip before
15	segmentation (observed live 2026-07-07, job f29d41d…).
16	
17	**Fix (**PR #512 MERGED to master 2026-07-07** as squash `20862d7`; dev commit
18	d53851b, branch auto-deleted):** a shared single decode pass
19	`frame_sampler.run_frame_sampling` + a `FrameSamplingValidator` base
20	(`sample_indices`/`_init_state`/`_consume`/`_finalize`); the registry decodes
21	ALL sampled frames ONCE up front and streams them to each validator's online
22	accumulator (one frame held at a time — do NOT cache ~125 frames; 4K = ~3 GB
23	OOM risk). Frames are byte-identical to the old seeks → decisions unchanged.
24	
25	**Non-obvious finding — DON'T "simplify" it to pure `grab()`:** the ticket
26	suggested a pure forward `grab()` pass with no seeking. Measured on the 1.4 GiB /
27	1080p60 / 29175-frame reference clip (`scratch/boxhill_highlights_stage/Adarsh v
28	Vasant.MP4`), pure grab-through was **38s — SLOWER than the 27–39s seek status
29	quo**, because `blur`/`relevance` sample right up to end-of-file so grabbing to
30	the last sample decodes the WHOLE video. The shipped **forward-hybrid** (grab
31	small gaps, ONE forward `POS_FRAMES` seek to skip big gaps) = **9.57s (~4×)**,
32	126 random seeks → 17 monotonic-forward. `grab()` is NOT "free" — it decodes.
33	
34	**Relationship to [[pr513-worker-skip-revalidation]] (PR #513):** file-DISJOINT
35	(verified 2026-07-07 via `gh pr view --json files`) — #512 = validators/ + CODE_MAP
36	+ test_frame_sampler; #513 = compute/ + orchestration.py + worker + test_api_cloud_dispatch
37	/ test_compute_backend / test_worker. **No git merge conflict** either order; the
38	"conflict" wording in the index refers to dev-time shared-checkout contention, not a
39	diff conflict. The two are **complementary**: #512 speeds up validation that runs;
40	#513 skips redundant re-validation on the worker dispatch path.
41	
42	The remaining floor is decoding `scene_cut`'s dense first-~50s region (needed to
43	hit its exact sample indices). Getting to literal "a few seconds" on 4K would
44	need keyframe-only sampling (`-skip_frame nokey`) or a downscaled proxy — both
45	change WHICH frames are sampled (and would break the same-indices regression in
46	`tests/test_frame_sampler.py`), so they're a separate, decision-affecting change.
47	**Tracked as issue #514** (follow-up to #512) — owner flagged it important to revisit.
48

### 12. user (2026-07-07T16:57:48.818Z)

1	"""Canonical file-identity hashing for the indexer.
2	
3	`video_id` is the MD5 of the *whole* video file. Historically each entrypoint
4	(`backend/main.py`, `run_eval.py`, `scripts/run_phase3_eval.py`, `scripts/run_process_and_stitch.py`)
5	defined its own `get_file_md5` with a different read-chunk size (64KB / 8KB / 1MB).
6	The hash values agreed (same bytes hashed), but the duplicated code was a footgun:
7	any future change to one copy would silently diverge the DB key.
8	
9	This module is the single source of truth. Import `compute_video_id` everywhere a
10	video needs to be keyed.
11	"""
12	
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	import hashlib
16	
17	# 64 KB matches the value used by the live pipeline (backend/main.py) before this
18	# was centralized. The chunk size does not affect the resulting digest — it only
19	# bounds memory use while streaming large files.
20	_CHUNK_SIZE = 65536
21	
22	
23	def compute_video_id(filepath: str) -> str:
24	    """Return the MD5 hex digest of the whole file — the canonical video_id.
25	
26	    Streams the file in fixed-size chunks so multi-gigabyte recordings never load
27	    into memory at once.
28	    """
29	    hasher = hashlib.md5()
30	    with open(filepath, "rb") as f:
31	        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(_CHUNK_SIZE), b""):
32	            hasher.update(chunk)
33	    return hasher.hexdigest()
34

47	# #466: serialize CPU/GPU-heavy LOCAL work — in-process segmentation and the ffmpeg compile stitch —
48	# so that raising the drain's worker count (to overlap I/O-bound REMOTE Cloud Run dispatch/poll) never
49	# accidentally parallelizes local work. A single self-hosted box serializes GPU work and Gemini is
50	# rate-fragile; a remote dispatch is a bucket-poll and never holds this lane, so N remote jobs overlap
51	# while local/compile jobs stay 1-at-a-time. It's a plain mutex (no nesting) → no deadlock; when the
52	# drain is serial (max_workers=1) it's an uncontended no-op = today's behaviour.

55	
56	def _finalize_reingest(db, video_id: str, segments, play_wm: int, cand_wm: int) -> None:
57	    """Commit a (re)segmentation with destroy-AFTER-confirm semantics.
58	
59	    On a non-empty run, drop the PRIOR rows (id <= the pre-run watermark) so only the
60	    fresh segments remain. On an empty run, drop the NEW partial rows (id > watermark) and
61	    keep the prior good results intact — a failed/empty re-run never destroys prior data.
62	    """
63	    if segments:
64	        db.prune_segments(video_id, play_upto=play_wm, cand_upto=cand_wm)
65	    else:
66	        db.prune_segments(video_id, play_after=play_wm, cand_after=cand_wm)

68	
69	def _validation_failure_message(failures: List[Any]) -> str:

73	        message = (getattr(failure, "message", None) or "").strip()
74	        details.append(f"{name}: {message}" if message else str(name))
75	    if not details:
76	        return "Video failed validation checks."
77	    return "Video failed validation: " + "; ".join(details)

86	    )

108	def _ingest_remote_segments(
109	    db, video_id: str, outputs_uri: str, storage_config: dict, *, max_rows: int = 20000
110	) -> int:
111	    """Read a cloud job's ``intervals.csv`` (start,end,shot_count,ending_reason) from the bucket and
112	    persist each rally via the SAME ``db.add_play_segment`` the in-process segmenters call — so
113	    ``/api/query`` + ``/api/compile`` read the rows with zero knowledge of where they were produced.
114	    Returns the count ingested. Mirrors ``cloud_run._read_json``'s fetch-to-a-real-dst pattern (cloud
115	    backends REQUIRE a dst). Hardened against a hostile/corrupt worker output: a malformed row is
116	    skipped, non-finite / overflowing values are rejected, the loop is bounded, and the temp dir is
117	    cleaned. May raise (missing/unreadable CSV, fetch error) — the caller treats that as a job failure
118	    and prunes any partial rows (destroy-AFTER-confirm)."""
119	    uri = f"{outputs_uri.rstrip('/')}/intervals.csv"
120	    ref = storage.parse_uri(uri)
121	    n = 0
122	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="cr-ingest-") as td:
123	        local = storage.fetch(
124	            uri, os.path.join(td, ref.name or "intervals.csv"), config=storage_config
125	        )
126	        with open(local, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
127	            for row in csv.DictReader(f):
128	                if n >= max_rows:
129	                    logger.warning(
130	                        "[API] cloud ingest hit the %d-row cap (%s) — ignoring the rest.",
131	                        max_rows,
132	                        uri,
133	                    )
134	                    break
135	                try:
136	                    start, end = float(row["start"]), float(row["end"])
137	                except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
138	                    continue
139	                if not (math.isfinite(start) and math.isfinite(end)):
140	                    continue  # reject inf/nan times rather than persist a junk rally
141	                meta: Dict[str, Any] = {
142	                    "source": "cloud_run",
143	                    "ending_reason": (row.get("ending_reason") or "").strip(),
144	                }
145	                sc = (row.get("shot_count") or "").strip()
146	                if sc:
147	                    try:
148	                        meta["shot_count"] = int(
149	                            float(sc)
150	                        )  # OverflowError on 'inf'/'1e400'
151	                    except (ValueError, OverflowError):
152	                        meta["shot_count"] = sc
153	                db.add_play_segment(video_id, start, end, metadata=meta)
154	                n += 1
155	    return n

158	# Trajectory segmenters whose IN-PROCESS detect needs model weights present on THIS box. A hosted

160	# GCSFuse-mounts them), so forcing compute="local" for one of these there can never succeed — the
161	# guardrail in _maybe_dispatch_remote fails such a request FAST instead of the cryptic deep
162	# "WASB weights not found". (Names are the registered segmenter identities; see backend/pipeline/segmenters.)
163	_WEIGHTS_BACKED_SEGMENTERS = frozenset({"wasb_hybrid", "tracknet_hybrid", "fusion_hybrid"})

165	
166	def _local_detector_weights_present(cfg, seg_name: str) -> bool:
167	    """Whether the trajectory weights the IN-PROCESS ``seg_name`` detector needs are present + readable
168	    on THIS box. Mirrors the native/WSL runners' resolution (env override → ``indexing.<det>.weights_path``)
169	    and the ``/api/capabilities`` probe, so the guardrail agrees with the healthcheck that would
170	    otherwise fail deep in the run. Fusion follows its configured substrate (WASB default / TrackNet).
171	    A non-weights segmenter returns True (nothing local to check)."""
172	    name = (seg_name or "").strip().lower()
173	    if name not in _WEIGHTS_BACKED_SEGMENTERS:
174	        return True
175	    idx = getattr(cfg, "indexing", None)
176	    substrate = (
177	        getattr(getattr(idx, "fusion", None), "substrate", "") or "wasb"
178	    ).strip().lower()
179	    if name == "tracknet_hybrid" or (name == "fusion_hybrid" and substrate == "tracknet"):
180	        path = getattr(getattr(idx, "tracknet", None), "weights_path", "") or ""
181	    else:  # wasb_hybrid, or fusion on the (default) WASB substrate
182	        path = (
183	            os.environ.get("WASB_WEIGHTS")
184	            or getattr(getattr(idx, "wasb", None), "weights_path", "")
185	            or ""
186	        )
187	    return bool(path) and os.path.exists(os.path.expanduser(path))

190	def _cloud_available(cfg) -> bool:
191	    """Whether THIS server can satisfy a per-request ``compute="cloud"`` dispatch (item 3).
192	
193	    True when the control plane is wired for Cloud Run: either the configured default target is
194	    already ``cloud_run`` (the operator chose cloud serving), or the Cloud Run env coordinates
195	    (``CLOUD_RUN_JOB`` + ``GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT``) and an output bucket (``storage.output_root``)
196	    are present so a forced per-request override can actually dispatch + collect a result.
197	
198	    Surfaced in ``/api/capabilities`` so the SPA only offers the cloud toggle when it would work."""
199	    deployment = getattr(cfg, "deployment", None)
200	    if (getattr(deployment, "compute_target", "") or "") == "cloud_run":
201	        return True
202	    has_job = bool(
203	        os.environ.get("CLOUD_RUN_JOB") and os.environ.get("GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT")
204	    )
205	    out_root = (getattr(getattr(cfg, "storage", None), "output_root", "") or "").strip()
206	    return bool(has_job and out_root)

208	
209	def _wait_progress_message(seg_name: str, elapsed_sec: float) -> str:
210	    """The live job.progress line while a cloud GPU run is in flight (#482). Honest and
211	    minute-granular: the control plane knows ELAPSED time, not frames — frame-level % comes
212	    with the worker-side heartbeat (the #482 follow-up). Under a minute reads as "starting"
213	    (queue/cold-start), so the very first tick already shows movement."""
214	    minutes = int(elapsed_sec) // 60
215	    if minutes < 1:
216	        return f"segmenting ({seg_name}) on cloud GPU — starting up"
217	    return f"segmenting ({seg_name}) on cloud GPU — {minutes} min elapsed"

220	def submit_time_video_id(input_ref, cfg) -> Optional[str]:
221	    """The video's md5 (the canonical ``video_id``) resolvable CHEAPLY at submit time — gs://
222	    object metadata only, one stat, no bytes (#484). ``None`` ⇒ unknown: local files keep

224	    ``md5Hash``. Never raises — a metadata miss must not block a submit."""
225	    if getattr(input_ref, "backend", "") != "gcs":
226	        return None

229	        return None

236	            getattr(input_ref, "uri", input_ref),

242	def _cached_process_payload(
243	    video_id: str, validation_results, segments, requested_compute
244	) -> Dict[str, Any]:

247	    return {

249	        "status": "success",

253	        ),
254	        "results": validation_results or [],
255	        "play_segments": segments,

257	        "compute": {"path": "cached", "requested": requested_compute},
258	    }

260	
261	def _read_bucket_json(uri: str, storage_config: dict) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
262	    """Fetch + parse one small JSON object (cloud backends require a real dst — the
263	    ``_ingest_remote_segments`` pattern). Raises on fetch/parse errors; callers decide."""
264	    ref = storage.parse_uri(uri)
265	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="cr-report-") as td:
266	        local = storage.fetch(
267	            uri, os.path.join(td, ref.name or "report.json"), config=storage_config
268	        )
269	        with open(local, encoding="utf-8") as f:
270	            loaded = json.load(f)
271	    return loaded if isinstance(loaded, dict) else None

273	
274	# One re-hydrate at a time per video_id (#492 review blocker 1): two concurrent cache hits
275	# from an empty DB must not both ingest intervals.csv — the loser waits, then finds the
276	# winner's rows in the DB and reuses them. Process-wide is enough: the CP is single-instance
277	# (maxScale=1, spec D8) and SQLite serializes cross-process writers anyway.
278	_REHYDRATE_LOCKS: Dict[str, threading.Lock] = {}
279	_REHYDRATE_LOCKS_GUARD = threading.Lock()
280	
281	
282	def _rehydrate_lock(video_id: str) -> threading.Lock:
283	    with _REHYDRATE_LOCKS_GUARD:
284	        return _REHYDRATE_LOCKS.setdefault(video_id, threading.Lock())
285	
286	
287	def probe_process_cache(db, cfg, video_id: str) -> bool:
288	    """READ-ONLY: does completed detection for ``video_id`` already exist (DB or bucket)?
289	
290	    Safe to call before the atomic submit claim — it writes nothing (#492 review blocker 1
291	    ordering). Used to decide whether a submit can skip the local-scratch fetch guard
292	    (blocker 2): a True probe means the request should never need CP scratch. Best-effort:
293	    errors report False (→ the normal dispatch path with its guards)."""
294	    try:
295	        existing = db.get_video(video_id)
296	        if (
297	            existing
298	            and existing.get("status") == "processed"
299	            and db.query_play_segments(video_id, {})
300	        ):
301	            return True
302	        sc = _storage_settings(cfg)
303	        if not sc or (sc.get("backend") or "local") == "local":
304	            return False
305	        output_root = (sc.get("output_root") or "").rstrip("/")
306	        if not output_root:
307	            return False
308	        report_uri = f"{output_root}/outputs/{video_id}/report.json"
309	        if not storage.exists(report_uri, config=sc):
310	            return False
311	        report = _read_bucket_json(report_uri, sc)
312	        return bool(report and report.get("status") == "success")
313	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — a failed probe degrades to the guarded dispatch path
314	        logger.warning(
315	            "[API] cache probe failed for %s — treating as a miss", video_id, exc_info=True
316	        )
317	        return False
318	
319	
320	def cached_process_result(
321	    db, cfg, video_id: str, video_path: str, requested_compute=None
322	) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
323	    """A ready-to-serve /api/process result when this video's detection ALREADY exists —
324	    the DB first, then the bucket (``outputs/<md5>/report.json`` with ``status=success``
325	    re-ingests via ``_ingest_remote_segments``): **the DB is a cache, the bucket is the
326	    source of truth** (#484 instant re-runs; the #485 re-hydrate mechanism). ``None`` ⇒ no
327	    completed work found (or the check failed) → the caller dispatches normally. The bucket
328	    branch mirrors the real cloud ingest exactly (watermarks + ``_finalize_reingest``), so a
329	    partial earlier ingest is replaced, never double-counted.
330	
331	    Concurrency (#492 review blocker 1): the whole check-then-ingest runs under a
332	    per-``video_id`` lock — a concurrent caller blocks, then finds the winner's rows via the
333	    in-lock DB re-check and reuses them (one segment set, ever). Callers must hold a CLAIMED
334	    job before invoking (writes happen post-claim)."""
335	    try:
336	        with _rehydrate_lock(video_id):
337	            existing = db.get_video(video_id)
338	            if existing and existing.get("status") == "processed":
339	                segs = db.query_play_segments(video_id, {})
340	                if segs:
341	                    return _cached_process_payload(
342	                        video_id,
343	                        existing.get("validation_results", []),
344	                        segs,
345	                        requested_compute,
346	                    )
347	            sc = _storage_settings(cfg)
348	            if not sc or (sc.get("backend") or "local") == "local":
349	                return None
350	            output_root = (sc.get("output_root") or "").rstrip("/")
351	            if not output_root:
352	                return None
353	            outputs_uri = f"{output_root}/outputs/{video_id}"
354	            report_uri = f"{outputs_uri}/report.json"
355	            if not storage.exists(report_uri, config=sc):
356	                return None
357	            report = _read_bucket_json(report_uri, sc)
358	            if not report or report.get("status") != "success":
359	                return None
360	            if not video_path:
361	                # Read-path re-hydrate (#485): a wiped DB has no source record. The report
362	                # carries the worker's --video-uri (P3); older reports fall back to the
363	                # staged-canonical videos/<md5>/ prefix; else "" — the segments still
364	                # recover, and the next same-md5 submit re-binds the truthful path via
365	                # add_video's upsert.
366	                video_path = str(report.get("video_uri") or "") or _first_object_under(
367	                    f"{output_root}/videos/{video_id}/", sc
368	                )
369	            play_wm, cand_wm = db.segment_watermarks(video_id)
370	            db.add_video(video_id, video_path, "processed", [])
371	            n = _ingest_remote_segments(db, video_id, outputs_uri, sc)
372	            if n <= 0:
373	                return None
374	            segments = [
375	                s
376	                for s in db.query_play_segments(video_id, {})
377	                if int(s.get("id", 0)) > play_wm
378	            ]
379	            _finalize_reingest(db, video_id, segments, play_wm, cand_wm)
380	            if not segments:
381	                return None
382	            logger.info(
383	                "[API] re-hydrated %d segments for video_id=%s from %s (submit-time cache hit)",
384	                n,
385	                video_id,
386	                outputs_uri,
387	            )
388	            return _cached_process_payload(video_id, [], segments, requested_compute)
389	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — cache misses must degrade to a normal dispatch
390	        logger.warning(
391	            "[API] submit-time cache check failed for %s — dispatching normally",
392	            video_id,
393	            exc_info=True,
394	        )
395	        return None
396	
397	
398	def _first_object_under(prefix_uri: str, storage_config: dict) -> str:
399	    """The first object URI under a bucket prefix, or "" — best-effort source recovery."""
400	    try:
401	        return next(iter(storage.list_uris(prefix_uri, config=storage_config)), "")
402	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — recovery nicety, never a failure
403	        return ""
404	
405	
406	_MD5_HEX_RE = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{32}")
407	
408	
409	def read_path_rehydrate(db, cfg, video_id: str) -> bool:
410	    """#485, the read half of "DB = cache, bucket = truth": before a rally READ reports
411	    nothing for an md5-shaped id, try re-hydrating the video from ``outputs/<md5>/`` — so
412	    ``?video=<md5>`` lands on grounded state after any restart/redeploy wipe. True ⇒ the DB
413	    has the video's rows now (re-query). Non-md5 ids return False without touching the
414	    bucket; every failure degrades to False (the read just reports what the DB has)."""
415	    vid = (video_id or "").strip().lower()
416	    if not _MD5_HEX_RE.fullmatch(vid):
417	        return False
418	    if not probe_process_cache(db, cfg, vid):
419	        return False
420	    return cached_process_result(db, cfg, vid, "") is not None
421	
422	
423	def _maybe_dispatch_remote(
424	    cfg,
425	    db,
426	    req: "ProcessRequest",
427	    video_id: str,
428	    seg_name: str,
429	    val_results,
430	    play_wm: int,
431	    cand_wm: int,
432	    notify,
433	) -> Optional[Tuple[Dict[str, Any], bool]]:
434	    """Cloud-serving (COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M6 → the API path): when ``deployment.compute_target
435	    == "cloud_run"``, run the heavy DETECT on a Cloud Run GPU Job and INGEST its rally intervals into
436	    THIS DB, so the rest of the flow (query → compile/local-stitch) is unchanged.
437	
438	    Returns ``(response_dict, ran)`` when the cloud path is taken — ``ran`` is whether the cloud job
439	    actually EXECUTED (``False`` for a pre-dispatch config rejection, so the observability report
440	    doesn't claim a fake segmentation run) — or ``None`` to fall through to the in-process segmenter
441	    (**DEFAULT-OFF**: ``get_compute_backend`` returns ``None`` for every non-``cloud_run`` target, so
442	    the in-process path stays byte-identical)."""
443	    from backend.compute import ComputeJobSpec, get_compute_backend
444	
445	    def _failed(msg: str, ran: bool) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], bool]:
446	        # Shape-match the in-process segmenter-fail branch + drop any partial NEW rows (id > play_wm);
447	        # prior good rows (id <= play_wm) are preserved (destroy-AFTER-confirm).
448	        db.prune_segments(video_id, play_after=play_wm, cand_after=cand_wm)
449	        # Provenance even on failure, and HONEST about what ran: `path` is "cloud_run" only when the
450	        # job actually dispatched + executed remotely (ran=True, e.g. a non-zero worker rc / ingest
451	        # error); a pre-dispatch reject (ran=False) ran NOWHERE → "not_run". `target` records the
452	        # intended backend, `requested` the picker choice, `dispatched` whether the remote job ran.
453	        return (
454	            {
455	                "video_id": video_id,
456	                "status": "failed",
457	                "message": msg,
458	                "results": [r.model_dump() for r in val_results],
459	                "play_segments": [],
460	                "compute": {
461	                    "path": "cloud_run" if ran else "not_run",
462	                    "requested": req.compute,
463	                    "target": "cloud_run",
464	                    "dispatched": ran,
465	                },
466	            },
467	            ran,
468	        )
469	
470	    # SPA compute-picker (item 3): a per-request choice overrides the server's configured default.
471	    # "local" forces the in-process segmenter; "cloud" forces a Cloud Run dispatch (guarded by
472	    # _cloud_available so we fail clearly rather than silently running local); None / unknown → default.
473	    choice = (req.compute or "").strip().lower()
474	    if choice and choice not in ("local", "cloud"):
475	        logger.warning(
476	            "[API] ignoring unknown compute=%r (expected 'local'|'cloud'); using server default.",
477	            req.compute,
478	        )
479	        choice = ""
480	    if choice == "local":
481	        # A hosted cloud-serving control-plane bakes NO detector weights into its image (only the
482	        # worker Cloud Run Job GCSFuse-mounts them), so forcing a weights-backed trajectory detector
483	        # to run IN-PROCESS here can never succeed — it dies deep in the segmenter with a cryptic
484	        # "WASB weights not found" (observed live: job f29d41d0…, 2026-07-07). Fail FAST with an
485	        # actionable message instead of that silent trap. A truly-local box (not cloud-capable), or a
486	        # cloud-wired box that ACTUALLY has the weights on disk, is unaffected — it runs in-process
487	        # below exactly as before (the weights-present check is the escape hatch).
488	        if (
489	            (seg_name or "").strip().lower() in _WEIGHTS_BACKED_SEGMENTERS
490	            and _cloud_available(cfg)
491	            and not _local_detector_weights_present(cfg, seg_name)
492	        ):
493	            return _failed(
494	                f"this hosted server can't run detection locally — the '{seg_name}' model weights "
495	                "live only on the cloud worker, not on the control-plane. Choose 'Run in cloud'.",
496	                False,
497	            )
498	        return None  # explicit "run on my machine" — in-process regardless of the server's target
499	    if choice == "cloud":
500	        if not _cloud_available(cfg):
501	            return _failed(
502	                "cloud-serving was requested but this server isn't configured for it "
503	                "(no Cloud Run target). Pick 'run on my machine', or configure cloud serving.",
504	                False,
505	            )
506	        compute = get_compute_backend(cfg, target_override="cloud_run")
507	    else:
508	        compute = get_compute_backend(
509	            cfg
510	        )  # server default (default-OFF unless cloud_run)
511	    if compute is None:
512	        return None  # default-OFF → run the in-process segmenter below (unchanged)
513	
514	    storage_cfg = getattr(cfg, "storage", None)
515	
516	    # The Cloud Run job fetches the input FROM THE BUCKET — it can't read a path local to this box.
517	    try:
518	        input_ref = storage.resolve_input_ref(req.video_path, storage_cfg)
519	    except ValueError as e:
520	        return _failed(
521	            f"cloud-serving: unresolvable input '{req.video_path}': {e}", False
522	        )
523	    if input_ref.backend == "local":
524	        # A browser upload lands on THIS box's local disk; the Cloud Run job (a different machine)
525	        # can't read it. Stage it UP to the input bucket first, then dispatch from there (#461). Only
526	        # possible when a CLOUD input_root is configured; without one we genuinely can't stage → fail.
527	        # Classify the ROOT ITSELF (parse_uri), not storage.input_backend: the staged URI below is
528	        # built by joining under input_root, and storage.put routes by parse_uri(staged_uri).backend —
529	        # so an explicit cloud root (gs://…, s3://…) stages fine with input_backend left at its
530	        # default, while a bare/local-looking root can't stage no matter what input_backend says
531	        # (resolve_input_uri only consults input_backend when input_root is EMPTY, and an empty root
532	        # gives us nothing to build the staged URI under).
533	        input_root = (getattr(storage_cfg, "input_root", "") or "").strip()
534	        try:
535	            root_backend = (
536	                storage.parse_uri(input_root).backend if input_root else "local"
537	            )
538	        except ValueError:  # unsupported scheme in input_root — nowhere real to stage to
539	            root_backend = "local"
540	        if root_backend == "local":
541	            return _failed(
542	                "cloud-serving needs a cloud input (e.g. gs://…), or a cloud storage.input_root to "
543	                "stage local uploads into; a path local to this machine can't be read by the Cloud "
544	                "Run job.",
545	                False,
546	            )
547	        local_src = input_ref.key  # resolved local path of the upload
548	        staged_uri = f"{input_root.rstrip('/')}/{video_id}/{os.path.basename(local_src)}"
549	        try:
550	            notify("staging upload to bucket")
551	            # Thread the active storage settings so backend-specific config survives (S3 endpoint_url/
552	            # region/addressing for R2·DO·MinIO; GCS project/client) — not a fresh default client.
553	            storage.put(
554	                local_src,
555	                staged_uri,
556	                config=(
557	                    storage_cfg.model_dump() if storage_cfg is not None else None
558	                ),
559	            )
560	        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a staging failure is a clean job failure, not a 500
561	            logger.error(
562	                "[API] cloud-serving: failed to stage %s -> %s: %s",
563	                local_src,
564	                staged_uri,
565	                e,
566	                exc_info=True,
567	            )
568	            return _failed(
569	                f"cloud-serving: failed to stage the uploaded file to {input_root}: {e}",
570	                False,
571	            )
572	        # Repoint BOTH the dispatch and the DB video record at the staged bucket object: the L4 job
573	        # DETECTs from it, and a later /api/compile re-fetches the same source (the local upload is
574	        # gone once this instance recycles). add_video is an UPSERT — it only updates filepath.
575	        req.video_path = staged_uri
576	        input_ref = storage.resolve_input_ref(staged_uri, storage_cfg)
577	        db.add_video(
578	            video_id, staged_uri, "processed", [r.model_dump() for r in val_results]
579	        )
580	    out_root = (getattr(storage_cfg, "output_root", "") or "").strip()
581	    if not out_root:
582	        return _failed(
583	            "cloud-serving needs storage.output_root set to a bucket (e.g. gs://your-bucket) "
584	            "— that's where the Cloud Run job writes the result.",
585	            False,
586	        )
587	
588	    notify("dispatching (cloud_run)")
589	    logger.info(
590	        "[API] cloud-serving: dispatching %s -> Cloud Run Job (out=%s)",
591	        req.video_path,
592	        out_root,
593	    )
594	    # Forward the engine's AI-handoff stance so the cloud job runs the SAME detection: with
595	    # skip_ai_handoff=True the WASB windows become rallies directly (no Gemini key needed); without
596	    # forwarding the cloud job would default to the handoff and yield 0 rallies when it has no key.
597	    # (player_pool / max_frames are still dropped on the cloud path — a separate documented follow-up.)
598	    overrides = []
599	    idx_cfg = getattr(cfg, "indexing", None)
600	    sk = getattr(idx_cfg, "skip_ai_handoff", None)
601	    if sk is not None:
602	        overrides.append(f"indexing.skip_ai_handoff={'true' if sk else 'false'}")
603	    # Forward the resolved WASB serving knobs so the PRESET governs the worker (#506/#507). The
604	    # worker Job has no DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE, so it does NOT apply the cloud-serving preset itself —
605	    # without forwarding, its decode_backend + GPU-feed tuning silently fall back to the model
606	    # defaults (cpu, batch 8) regardless of what the control-plane resolved. decode_backend is
607	    # cuda-only (validator-enforced) and requires a worker image that understands --decode-backend
608	    # (≥ #507); batch_size/prefetch_batches are forwarded only when set (None = the parity default).
609	    wasb_cfg = getattr(idx_cfg, "wasb", None)
610	    if wasb_cfg is not None:
611	        decode_backend = getattr(wasb_cfg, "decode_backend", None)
612	        if decode_backend:
613	            overrides.append(f"indexing.wasb.decode_backend={decode_backend}")
614	        batch_size = getattr(wasb_cfg, "batch_size", None)
615	        if batch_size:
616	            overrides.append(f"indexing.wasb.batch_size={batch_size}")
617	        prefetch = getattr(wasb_cfg, "prefetch_batches", None)
618	        if prefetch:
619	            overrides.append(f"indexing.wasb.prefetch_batches={prefetch}")
620	    # Tell the worker to SKIP its redundant re-validation: THIS control-plane is the authoritative
621	    # gate — it already ran the validators with its resolved config just above (only a PASS reaches
622	    # here; a failure returned "failed" before dispatch). A worker re-run would decode the video a
623	    # second time and, because its config isn't fully forwarded, can resolve DIFFERENT thresholds and
624	    # REJECT a clip this gate already passed (observed 2026-07-07: passed at min_blur_threshold=8.0,
625	    # then worker-rejected at its default 20.0 after a successful GPU dispatch).
626	    spec = ComputeJobSpec(
627	        video_uri=req.video_path,
628	        out_uri=out_root,
629	        segmenter=seg_name,
630	        config_overrides=tuple(overrides),
631	        skip_validation=True,
632	    )
633	    try:
634	        # Dispatch the Cloud Run Job + bucket-poll to completion. on_wait is the live
635	        # heartbeat (#482): job.progress moves on every poll tick instead of freezing at
636	        # "segmenting" for the whole GPU run (the 2026-07-05 silent 22-min spinner) — which
637	        # also gives the SPA a stall signal to time out on, instead of a blind wall clock.
638	        result = compute.run_infer(
639	            spec,
640	            on_wait=lambda elapsed: notify(_wait_progress_message(seg_name, elapsed)),
641	        )
642	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — any dispatch/poll error is a clean job failure, not a 500
643	        logger.error("[API] cloud dispatch error: %s", e, exc_info=True)
644	        return _failed(f"cloud dispatch error: {e}", True)
645	    if result.returncode != 0:
646	        return _failed(f"cloud job failed (returncode {result.returncode}).", True)
647	    if result.video_id and result.video_id != video_id:
648	        # The worker re-hashes the bucket bytes; a divergence means the input changed between this
649	        # box's hash and the worker's — ingest under the LOCAL id but flag it so it's diagnosable.
650	        logger.warning(
651	            "[API] cloud video_id %s != local %s — input bytes differ between the API hash "
652	            "and the worker hash; ingesting under the local id.",
653	            result.video_id,
654	            video_id,
655	        )
656	
657	    notify("ingesting (cloud_run)")
658	    storage_dict = (
659	        storage_cfg.model_dump()  # type: ignore[union-attr]  # hasattr-guarded; mypy can't narrow
660	        if hasattr(storage_cfg, "model_dump")
661	        else {}
662	    )
663	    try:
664	        _ingest_remote_segments(db, video_id, result.outputs_uri, storage_dict)
665	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a missing/partial/corrupt result must not 500 or leak rows
666	        logger.error("[API] cloud ingest failed: %s", e, exc_info=True)
667	        return _failed(
668	            f"cloud-serving: could not read the result from {result.outputs_uri}: {e}",
669	            True,
670	        )
671	
672	    # Only the NEW cloud rows (id > the pre-run watermark); _finalize_reingest then drops the old ones.
673	    segments = [
674	        s for s in db.query_play_segments(video_id, {}) if int(s.get("id", 0)) > play_wm
675	    ]
676	    _finalize_reingest(db, video_id, segments, play_wm, cand_wm)
677	    # Compute-path PROVENANCE: the GCP-verifiable proof the cloud path ran. `execution` is the real
678	    # Cloud Run execution name (cross-check: gcloud run jobs executions describe <execution>).
679	    provenance = {
680	        "path": "cloud_run",
681	        "requested": req.compute,
682	        "job": os.environ.get("CLOUD_RUN_JOB", "rally-worker"),
683	        "region": os.environ.get("CLOUD_RUN_REGION", "asia-south1"),
684	        "execution": result.execution,
685	        "outputs_uri": result.outputs_uri,
686	    }
687	    logger.info(
688	        "[COMPUTE] cloud_run dispatched: execution=%s job=%s region=%s out=%s video_id=%s",
689	        result.execution,
690	        provenance["job"],
691	        provenance["region"],
692	        result.outputs_uri,
693	        video_id,
694	    )
695	    return (
696	        {
697	            "video_id": video_id,
698	            "status": "success",
699	            "message": f"Successfully indexed {len(segments)} play segments (cloud_run / '{seg_name}').",
700	            "results": [r.model_dump() for r in val_results],
701	            "play_segments": segments,
702	            "compute": provenance,
703	        },
704	        True,
705	    )
706	
707	
708	def _execute_processing(
709	    config=None, db=None, req=None, progress=None
710	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
711	    """The full hash → validate → segment → report pipeline for one video.
712	
713	    Accepts explicit ``config``/``db`` (the new app-factory pattern) OR reads the
714	    module-level ``global_config``/``db_instance`` (backward-compatible with existing
715	    tests that pass only ``req``). Route handlers always pass explicit params; older
716	    test code that calls ``_execute_processing(req)`` still works.
717	
718	    ``progress`` is an optional callable(stage: str) used to surface coarse job progress.
719	    Raises on unexpected internal errors — the caller records those as a job failure
720	    """
721	    import backend.main as _main  # call-time seam: main's globals/model stay patchable
722	
723	    # Backward-compatible shim: if called as _execute_processing(req) (old pattern),
724	    # shift positional args — req arrives in position 0 (config), progress in position 1 (db).
725	    if config is not None and not isinstance(config, AppConfig):
726	        # Old signature: _execute_processing(req, progress=None)
727	        if req is None and db is not None and isinstance(db, _main.ProcessRequest):
728	            req = db
729	            db = None
730	        elif req is None and isinstance(config, _main.ProcessRequest):
731	            req = config
732	            config = None
733	    # Resolve config/db from module globals when not passed explicitly
734	    if config is None:
735	        config = _main.global_config
736	    if db is None:
737	        db = _main.db_instance
738	
739	    notify = progress or (lambda stage: None)
740	
741	    notify("hashing")
742	    # M2: resolve the input to a LOCAL path. A bare/local path is returned unchanged (identity —
743	    # byte-identical to today); a bucket/Drive URI is fetched to scratch first. The original
744	    # req.video_path (the source URI) is kept for the DB record + report; only the file-reading
745	    # consumers (hash / validators / segmenter) use the resolved local path.
746	    local_video = ""
747	    try:
748	        local_video = storage.acquire_local_input(
749	            req.video_path, getattr(config, "storage", None)
750	        )
751	        video_id = _main.compute_video_id(local_video)
752	    except Exception:
753	        storage.cleanup_acquired_local_input(
754	            req.video_path, local_video, getattr(config, "storage", None)
755	        )
756	        raise
757	    try:
758	        existing_video = db.get_video(video_id)
759	    except Exception:
760	        storage.cleanup_acquired_local_input(
761	            req.video_path, local_video, getattr(config, "storage", None)
762	        )
763	        raise
764	    was_reingest = existing_video is not None
765	
766	    # typed AppConfig: attribute access for the default segmenter (with safe fallback)
767	    default_seg = getattr(
768	        getattr(config, "indexing", None), "default_segmenter", "motion"
769	    )
770	    seg_name = req.segmenter or default_seg
771	    segmenter_actual = None
772	    segmentation_ran = False
773	    val_results: List[Any] = []
774	    val_context: Dict[str, Any] = {}
775	    # Compute-path PROVENANCE (the validation method): which backend actually ran the DETECT —
776	    # "in_process" once the local segmenter is reached, set on the response as "cloud_run" by
777	    # _maybe_dispatch_remote when it dispatches. None ⇒ nothing ran (validation/config fail).
778	    compute_actual: Optional[str] = None
779	    response: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
780	    try:
781	        if (
782	            existing_video
783	            and existing_video.get("status") == "processed"
784	            and not req.force
785	        ):
786	            cached_segments = db.query_play_segments(video_id, {})
787	            if cached_segments:
788	                logger.info(
789	                    "[API] Reusing cached segmentation for video_id=%s; force=true bypasses cache.",
790	                    video_id,
791	                )
792	                response = _cached_process_payload(
793	                    video_id,
794	                    existing_video.get("validation_results", []),
795	                    cached_segments,
796	                    getattr(req, "compute", None),
797	                )
798	                return response
799	
800	        # 1. Run pluggable validators (with-context variant surfaces ffprobe_meta for the report)
801	        notify("validating")
802	        logger.info(f"Running validations for {req.video_path}...")
803	        val_results, val_context = registry.run_all_with_context(local_video, config)
804	        failures = [r for r in val_results if not r.passed]
805	
806	        if failures:
807	            # add_video is an UPSERT (no cascade) — a failed re-validation only updates
808	            # status; it no longer destroys the video's prior good segments.
809	            db.add_video(
810	                video_id,
811	                req.video_path,
812	                "failed",
813	                [r.model_dump() for r in val_results],
814	            )
815	            response = {
816	                "video_id": video_id,
817	                "status": "failed",
818	                "message": _validation_failure_message(failures),
819	                "results": [r.model_dump() for r in val_results],
820	            }
821	            return response
822	
823	        # 2. Run segmenter & indexer
824	        logger.info("[API] Video passed checks. Segmenting and indexing...")
825	        db.add_video(
826	            video_id, req.video_path, "processed", [r.model_dump() for r in val_results]
827	        )
828	
829	        segmenter_cls = segmenter_registry.get(seg_name)
830	        if not segmenter_cls:
831	            # Submit-time validation already 400s unknown names; this is the defensive
832	            # in-worker path (e.g. config default pointing at an unregistered segmenter).
833	            available = list(segmenter_registry.list_available().keys())
834	            response = {
835	                "video_id": video_id,
836	                "status": "failed",
837	                "message": f"Segmenter '{seg_name}' not found. Available segmenters: {available}",
838	                "results": [r.model_dump() for r in val_results],
839	                "play_segments": [],
840	            }
841	            return response
842	
843	        logger.info(f"[API] Using segmenter: {seg_name}")
844	        notify(f"segmenting ({seg_name})")
845	        segmenter_actual = seg_name
846	        # Destroy-AFTER-confirm: remember the pre-run rows, then keep new + drop old only
847	        # if the run yields segments; otherwise drop the new partial rows and keep the old.
848	        play_wm, cand_wm = db.segment_watermarks(video_id)
849	        # COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M6 → API: with deployment.compute_target=cloud_run, run the heavy
850	        # DETECT on a Cloud Run GPU Job + ingest its rallies HERE so query/compile are unchanged.
851	        # DEFAULT-OFF: returns None for every non-cloud target → the in-process segmenter below runs
852	        # byte-identically. (player_pool/max_frames aren't threaded to the worker `infer` CLI yet, so
853	        # they're dropped on the cloud path in this slice — a documented follow-up.)
854	        remote = _maybe_dispatch_remote(
855	            config, db, req, video_id, seg_name, val_results, play_wm, cand_wm, notify
856	        )
857	        if remote is not None:
858	            response, segmentation_ran = (
859	                remote  # `ran` = did the cloud job actually execute (report)
860	            )
861	            return response  # the cloud branch already stamped response["compute"] (path=cloud_run)
862	        # In-process from here on — record the actual path for the provenance stamp in `finally`.
863	        compute_actual = "in_process"
864	        segmenter = segmenter_cls(db, config)
865	        try:
866	            # Serialize the CPU/GPU-heavy in-process detection (see _LOCAL_COMPUTE_LANE). A remote
867	            # dispatch never reaches here (it returned above), so remote polls still overlap freely.
868	            with _LOCAL_COMPUTE_LANE:
869	                result = segmenter.process_video(
870	                    video_id, local_video, req.player_pool, req.max_frames
871	                )
872	        except Exception:
873	            # A raising segmenter must not leave half-written rows: restore the pre-run
874	            # state (same destroy-after-confirm contract as the Failure branch), then let
875	            # the job worker record the failure.
876	            db.prune_segments(video_id, play_after=play_wm, cand_after=cand_wm)
877	            raise
878	        segmentation_ran = True
879	
880	        if isinstance(result, Failure):
881	            logger.error(f"[API] Segmenter failed: {result.message}")
882	            db.prune_segments(video_id, play_after=play_wm, cand_after=cand_wm)
883	            response = {
884	                "video_id": video_id,
885	                "status": "failed",
886	                "message": f"Segmenter '{seg_name}' failed: {result.message}",
887	                "results": [r.model_dump() for r in val_results],
888	                "play_segments": [],
889	            }
890	            return response
891	
892	        segments = result.value if hasattr(result, "value") else result
893	        notify("finalizing")
894	        _finalize_reingest(db, video_id, segments, play_wm, cand_wm)
895	
896	        response = {
897	            "video_id": video_id,
898	            "status": "success",
899	            "message": f"Successfully indexed {len(segments)} play segments using '{seg_name}' segmenter.",  # type: ignore[arg-type]  # hasattr-unwrap above; Success arm has .value
900	            "results": [r.model_dump() for r in val_results],
901	            "play_segments": segments,
902	        }
903	        return response
904	    finally:
905	        # Compute-path PROVENANCE stamp (the validation method): the cloud branch already set
906	        # response["compute"] (path=cloud_run + the Cloud Run execution); for every other path stamp
907	        # the actual path here so the job result PROVES which backend ran (in_process / not_run) and
908	        # what the picker REQUESTED — a silent fallback (requested cloud, ran local) is then visible.
909	        if isinstance(response, dict):
910	            response.setdefault(
911	                "compute",
912	                {
913	                    "path": compute_actual or "not_run",
914	                    "requested": getattr(req, "compute", None),
915	                },
916	            )
917	        prov = response.get("compute", {}) if isinstance(response, dict) else {}
918	        logger.info(
919	            "[COMPUTE] video_id=%s requested=%s actual=%s dispatched=%s status=%s",
920	            video_id,
921	            getattr(req, "compute", None),
922	            prov.get("path"),
923	            prov.get("dispatched"),
924	            (response or {}).get("status") if isinstance(response, dict) else None,
925	        )
926	        # Always emit the per-video observability report (next to the video, or to
927	        # report.output_dir). Never raises. Surface the paths in the response. (The compute-path
928	        # proof lives on the job result + the [COMPUTE] log, not the report — obsreport's record_run
929	        # doesn't surface arbitrary run signals.)
930	        paths = _main.emit_indexer_report(

932	            video_id=video_id,

934	            # video via os.path.abspath(video_path), and a remote URI (gs://, gcs://, local://)
935	            # collapses to a literal scheme dir at the repo root (see #397). The source URI is
936	            # still preserved in DB provenance via db.add_video.
937	            video_path=local_video,
938	            config=_report_config_for_input(config, req.video_path),
939	            val_results=val_results,
940	            val_context=val_context,
941	            segmenter_requested=req.segmenter,
942	            segmenter_actual=segmenter_actual,

952	
953	def _remote_input_size_bytes(input_ref, storage_config) -> Optional[int]:
954	    """Best-effort size (bytes) of a remote storage source, or ``None`` if unknowable.
955	
956	    Used only to pre-reserve scratch for a fetch-to-local (P2). Never raises: a stat that needs a
957	    missing cloud SDK, the network, or a credential returns ``None`` so the free-space guard degrades
958	    to a no-op rather than blocking a job it cannot measure."""
959	    try:
960	        sc = (
961	            storage_config.model_dump()
962	            if hasattr(storage_config, "model_dump")
963	            else (storage_config or {})
964	        )
965	        backend = storage.get_storage(input_ref.backend, config=sc)
966	        return int(backend.stat(input_ref).size)
967	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - measuring is best-effort; inability must not block
968	        logger.debug(
969	            "free-space: could not stat remote source %s: %s", input_ref.uri, e
970	        )
971	        return None
972	
973	
974	class _CompileError(Exception):
975	    """A submit-time-validatable compile problem — carries the HTTP status + detail so the API path
976	    maps it to an HTTPException (fast 4xx) and the worker path maps it to a failed-job result."""
977	
978	    def __init__(self, status_code: int, detail: str):
979	        super().__init__(detail)
980	        self.status_code = status_code
981	        self.detail = detail
982	
983	
984	def _resolve_compile(
985	    db, cfg, video_id: str, segment_ids: List[int], output_filename: str
986	):
987	    """Shared compile resolution — run at SUBMIT (fast-fail 4xx) AND in the WORKER (re-resolve, since a
988	    row could change between submit and run). Verifies the video exists, the requested segments match
989	    and survive the ``stitching.min_shot_count`` floor (segments WITHOUT a known shot_count are exempt
990	    so manual/legacy picks can't silently vanish), and the output name is traversal-safe. Raises
991	    ``_CompileError(404/400)``. Returns ``(video_row, kept_segments, safe_out_name)``."""
992	    video = db.get_video(video_id)
993	    if not video:
994	        raise _CompileError(404, "Indexed video record not found.")
995	    # Reject path traversal / absolute names (os.path.join would otherwise write anywhere on disk).
996	    try:
997	        out_name = safe_output_filename(output_filename)
998	    except ValueError as e:
999	        raise _CompileError(400, str(e)) from e
1000	
1001	    all_segments = db.query_play_segments(video_id, {})
1002	    matched = [s for s in all_segments if s["id"] in segment_ids]
1003	    if not matched:
1004	        raise _CompileError(400, "No matching play segments found to stitch.")
1005	
1006	    stitching = getattr(cfg, "stitching", None) or StitchingConfig()
1007	    kept = []
1008	    for s in matched:
1009	        meta = s.get("metadata") or {}
1010	        sc = meta.get("shot_count") if isinstance(meta, dict) else None
1011	        try:
1012	            if sc is not None and int(sc) < stitching.min_shot_count:
1013	                continue
1014	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
1015	            pass  # unparseable count -> treat as unknown, keep
1016	        kept.append(s)
1017	    if len(kept) < len(matched):
1018	        logger.info(
1019	            "[compile] stitching.min_shot_count=%s: dropped %d/%d segments below the floor.",
1020	            stitching.min_shot_count,
1021	            len(matched) - len(kept),
1022	            len(matched),
1023	        )
1024	    if not kept:
1025	        raise _CompileError(
1026	            400,
1027	            f"All {len(matched)} matching segments fall below "
1028	            f"stitching.min_shot_count={stitching.min_shot_count}.",
1029	        )
1030	    return video, kept, out_name
1031	
1032	
1033	def reel_object_uri(cfg, video_id: str, name: str) -> str:
1034	    """The bucket URI a compiled reel is mirrored to for a direct signed-URL download (#463), or "" when
1035	    the output isn't a cloud bucket (truly-local → serve the local file). Shared by the compile
1036	    mirror-upload and the /api/highlights redirect: ``<output_root>/highlights/<video_id>/<name>``."""
1037	    root = (getattr(getattr(cfg, "storage", None), "output_root", "") or "").strip()
1038	    if not root or "://" not in root:
1039	        return ""
1040	    return f"{root.rstrip('/')}/highlights/{video_id}/{name}"
1041	
1042	
1043	def _storage_settings(cfg) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
1044	    """The active storage settings dict (per-backend config for the storage layer — S3 endpoint/region,
1045	    GCS project/client/signed_url_ttl), or None. Threaded into storage calls so a non-default backend
1046	    isn't silently constructed as a fresh default."""
1047	    sc = getattr(cfg, "storage", None)
1048	    return sc.model_dump() if sc is not None else None
1049	
1050	
1051	def signed_reel_url(cfg, video_id: str, name: str) -> Optional[str]:
1052	    """A short-lived signed URL for a reel mirrored to the output bucket, so /api/highlights can
1053	    307-redirect off the app's response path (bypassing Cloud Run's ~32 MiB cap + the ephemeral local
1054	    /tmp; #463). None → no bucket copy → serve the local file. Uses the active storage config for BOTH
1055	    the existence check and the signing (extracted here to keep backend/main.py lean — P23/P24)."""
1056	    reel_uri = reel_object_uri(cfg, video_id, name)
1057	    if not reel_uri:
1058	        return None
1059	    settings = _storage_settings(cfg)
1060	    if storage.exists(reel_uri, config=settings):
1061	        return storage.signed_url(reel_uri, config=settings, method="GET")
1062	    return None
1063	
1064	
1065	def _execute_compile(cfg, db, job: Dict[str, Any], progress=None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
1066	    """Worker handler for a ``kind='compile'`` job (#329): re-resolve the segments, stitch the reel,
1067	    and return the {status, filepath, download_url} result the SPA polls for. Returns a
1068	    ``status='failed'`` result for an EXPECTED problem (missing video / no segments / a stitch error)
1069	    so the SPA always gets a clean verdict instead of a worker-crash error."""
1070	    import backend.main as _main  # call-time seam: VideoCompiler stays patchable on backend.main
1071	
1072	    notify = progress or (lambda stage: None)
1073	    params = json.loads(job.get("params") or "{}")
1074	    video_id = job.get("video_id") or ""
1075	    segment_ids = params.get("segment_ids") or []
1076	    locale = params.get("locale")
1077	
1078	    notify("resolving")
1079	    try:
1080	        video, kept, out_name = _resolve_compile(
1081	            db, cfg, video_id, segment_ids, params.get("output_filename", "")
1082	        )
1083	    except _CompileError as e:
1084	        return {"status": "failed", "message": e.detail, "video_id": video_id}
1085	
1086	    stitching = getattr(cfg, "stitching", None) or StitchingConfig()
1087	    output_dir = (
1088	        getattr(getattr(cfg, "output", None), "output_dir", "output") or "output"
1089	    )
1090	    # Localize the branding watermark to the UI locale the SPA rendered in (recorded on the job);
1091	    # unknown/absent locale keeps the configured default unchanged.
1092	    localized_watermark = localize_watermark(stitching.watermark, locale)
1093	    compiler = _main.VideoCompiler(
1094	        output_dir,
1095	        begin_padding=stitching.begin_padding,
1096	        end_padding=stitching.end_padding,
1097	        watermark=localized_watermark,
1098	    )
1099	    time_pairs = [(s["start_time"], s["end_time"]) for s in kept]
1100	
1101	    notify("stitching")
1102	    local_src = ""
1103	    try:
1104	        # The stored `filepath` is the SOURCE ref (gs://, s3://, gdrive://, or a local path). Resolve to
1105	        # a LOCAL file the stitcher can read — identity for a local path, fetch-to-scratch for a URI (the
1106	        # same seam the process path uses); without it, compiling a bucket-ingested video can't open gs://.
1107	        local_src = storage.acquire_local_input(
1108	            video["filepath"], getattr(cfg, "storage", None)
1109	        )
1110	        # The ffmpeg stitch is CPU-heavy — serialize it so a raised drain worker count (for remote
1111	        # dispatch) doesn't run multiple compiles at once on the box (see _LOCAL_COMPUTE_LANE).
1112	        with _LOCAL_COMPUTE_LANE:
1113	            out_path = compiler.compile_highlights(local_src, time_pairs, out_name)
1114	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a stitch/ffmpeg failure is a clean job FAILURE, not a crash
1115	        logger.error(
1116	            "[compile] stitching failed for video_id=%s: %s", video_id, e, exc_info=True
1117	        )
1118	        return {
1119	            "status": "failed",
1120	            "message": f"Highlight stitching failed: {e}",
1121	            "video_id": video_id,
1122	        }
1123	    finally:
1124	        if local_src:
1125	            storage.cleanup_acquired_local_input(
1126	                video["filepath"], local_src, getattr(cfg, "storage", None)
1127	            )
1128	
1129	    # #463: mirror the reel to the output bucket so /api/highlights can 307 to a signed URL — Cloud Run
1130	    # caps a buffered response at ~32 MiB (a real reel is bigger) and the local /tmp copy dies when the
1131	    # instance recycles. A mirror-upload failure must NOT fail an otherwise-good stitch (the endpoint
1132	    # still falls back to the local file on the same instance).
1133	    reel_uri = reel_object_uri(cfg, video_id, out_name)
1134	    if reel_uri:
1135	        try:
1136	            notify("uploading reel")
1137	            # Thread the active storage settings so the configured backend (S3 endpoint/region, GCS
1138	            # project/client) is used, not a fresh default.
1139	            storage.put(out_path, reel_uri, config=_storage_settings(cfg))
1140	        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort mirror; local stitch already succeeded
1141	            logger.warning("[compile] could not mirror reel to %s: %s", reel_uri, e)
1142	
1143	    # `filepath` is server-local (not browser-reachable); also return the download URL so the SPA needn't
1144	    # hand-build it (P6 / PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §4.3).
1145	    download_url = f"/api/highlights/{quote(video_id)}/{quote(out_name)}"
1146	    notify("finished")
1147	    return {
1148	        "status": "success",
1149	        "filepath": out_path,
1150	        "download_url": download_url,
1151	        "video_id": video_id,
1152	    }
1153

14	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
15	
16	
17	class ValidationResult(BaseModel):
18	    validator_name: str
19	    passed: bool
20	    message: str
21	    details: dict[str, Any] = {}
22	
23	
24	class VideoValidator(ABC):
25	    @property
26	    @abstractmethod
27	    def name(self) -> str:
28	        """Unique identifier for this validator."""
29	        pass
30	
31	    @property
32	    @abstractmethod
33	    def description(self) -> str:
34	        """Human-readable explanation of what this validator checks."""
35	        pass
36	
37	    @abstractmethod
38	    def validate(
39	        self, video_path: str, config: Any, context: dict[str, Any]
40	    ) -> ValidationResult:
41	        """
42	        Executes validation.
43	        - video_path: Local path to the MP4 file.
44	        - config: AppConfig (typed) — callers pass the typed model; attribute access
45	          preferred over .get() dict chains.
46	        - context: Dynamic context dict shared across validators (stores shared values
47	          like ffprobe metadata).
48	        """
49	        pass
50	
51	
52	class FrameSamplingValidator(VideoValidator):
53	    """A validator whose work is sampling decoded frames.
54	
55	    The three frame-decoding validators (scene_cut, blur, relevance) used to each
56	    open their own ``cv2.VideoCapture`` and seek to their sample frames — ~125
57	    random ``POS_FRAMES`` seeks across three decode opens, which cost minutes on
58	    long-GOP 4K clips (see ``frame_sampler``). Subclasses instead declare *which*
59	    frame indices they want (:meth:`sample_indices`) and reduce those frames
60	    online (:meth:`_init_state` / :meth:`_consume`), so the registry can decode
61	    the whole set **once** in a single shared forward pass.
62	
63	    :meth:`validate` uses the shared pass' result when the registry ran it; when
64	    a subclass is invoked standalone (direct ``.validate`` call, no shared
65	    context) it decodes its own indices in one pass via the same engine. Either
66	    way the frames — and therefore the pass/fail decision — are identical to the
67	    old per-validator seeks.
68	    """
69	
70	    @abstractmethod
71	    def sample_indices(self, total_frames: int, fps: float) -> List[int]:
72	        """Absolute frame indices this validator samples, in ascending order.
73	
74	        Pure and deterministic in ``(total_frames, fps)`` — the shared pass unions
75	        these across validators to drive one decode, and the regression suite pins
76	        them to the legacy per-validator seek arithmetic.
77	        """
78	        ...
79	
80	    @abstractmethod
81	    def _init_state(self, config: Any) -> Any:
82	        """Fresh accumulator for one run (may pre-resolve config-derived params)."""
83	        ...
84	
85	    @abstractmethod
86	    def _consume(self, state: Any, frame_idx: int, frame: Any) -> None:
87	        """Fold one sampled frame (BGR ndarray) into ``state``. Called in index order."""
88	        ...
89	
90	    @abstractmethod
91	    def _finalize(
92	        self, state: Any, meta: SampleMeta, config: Any, context: dict[str, Any]
93	    ) -> ValidationResult:
94	        """Render the ValidationResult from the accumulated ``state`` + decode meta."""
95	        ...
96	
97	    def validate(
98	        self, video_path: str, config: Any, context: dict[str, Any]
99	    ) -> ValidationResult:
100	        states = context.get(FRAME_STATES_KEY)
101	        if states is not None and self.name in states:
102	            # Shared single pass already decoded and reduced our frames.
103	            meta = context[FRAME_META_KEY]
104	            state = states[self.name]
105	        else:
106	            # Standalone: decode just this validator's indices in one pass.
107	            meta, own_states = run_frame_sampling(video_path, [self], config)
108	            state = own_states[self.name]
109	        return self._finalize(state, meta, config, context)
110	
111	
112	class ValidationRegistry:
113	    def __init__(self):
114	        self._validators: List[VideoValidator] = []
115	
116	    def register(self, validator: VideoValidator):
117	        """Registers a new validator."""
118	        self._validators.append(validator)
119	
120	    def run_all_with_context(self, video_path: str, config: Any):
121	        """Runs all validators and returns ``(results, context)``.
122	
123	        The per-run ``context`` accumulates shared values (notably
124	        ``context['ffprobe_meta']`` produced by FormatValidator). ``run_all``
125	        discards it; callers that want media metadata for an observability report
126	        use this variant instead of re-probing.
127	        """
128	        results = []
129	        context: dict[
130	            str, Any
131	        ] = {}  # Shared context (e.g. for caching ffprobe outputs)
132	        self._prepare_frame_samples(video_path, config, context)
133	        for val in self._validators:
134	            try:
135	                res = val.validate(video_path, config, context)
136	                results.append(res)
137	            except Exception as e:
138	                results.append(
139	                    ValidationResult(
140	                        validator_name=val.name,
141	                        passed=False,
142	                        message=f"Validator threw an unexpected error: {str(e)}",
143	                    )
144	                )
145	        return results, context
146	
147	    def _prepare_frame_samples(
148	        self, video_path: str, config: Any, context: dict[str, Any]
149	    ) -> None:
150	        """Decode every frame-sampling validator's frames in ONE shared pass.
151	
152	        Populates ``context`` with the shared decode meta + per-validator
153	        accumulator state; each :class:`FrameSamplingValidator` then finalizes
154	        from that instead of opening its own capture. Best-effort: any failure
155	        here is swallowed so validators transparently fall back to a standalone
156	        pass (the run must never crash on a decode hiccup)."""
157	        samplers = [
158	            v for v in self._validators if isinstance(v, FrameSamplingValidator)
159	        ]
160	        if not samplers:
161	            return
162	        try:
163	            meta, states = run_frame_sampling(video_path, samplers, config)
164	        except Exception:
165	            logger.exception(
166	                "Shared frame-sampling pass failed for %s; validators will fall "
167	                "back to standalone decode.",
168	                video_path,
169	            )
170	            return
171	        context[FRAME_META_KEY] = meta
172	        context[FRAME_STATES_KEY] = states
173	
174	    def run_all(self, video_path: str, config: Any) -> List[ValidationResult]:
175	        """Runs all registered validators in sequence, accumulating results."""
176	        results, _ = self.run_all_with_context(video_path, config)
177	        return results
178	
179	
180	# Shared global registry
181	registry = ValidationRegistry()

3	This module defines the canonical, validated, typed configuration for the

9	from __future__ import annotations
10	
11	from typing import Any, List, Literal, Optional
12
